agot_di_rusbar_eye_close_effect = {
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_open
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_target
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_age
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	clear_variable_list = agot_di_rusbar_eye_heritages
	clear_variable_list = agot_di_rusbar_eye_religions
}
agot_di_rusbar_eye_open_effect = {
	agot_di_rusbar_eye_close_effect = yes
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_target value = scope:recipient }
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture value = scope:recipient.culture }
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith value = scope:recipient.faith }
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_age value = 16 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender value = 50 }
	every_culture_global = {
		# One representative per heritage, as in DI; lists belong to this player.
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_list_culture
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_operator = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						any_in_list = {
							variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_heritages
							has_same_culture_heritage = scope:agot_di_rusbar_list_culture
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_heritages target = scope:agot_di_rusbar_list_culture }
			}
		}
	}
	every_religion_global = {
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_operator = { add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_religions target = prev } }
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_open value = yes }
}

		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_admiral_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_admiral_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_stewardship_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_admiral_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = admiral_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = admiral_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_archer_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }

	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_archer_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_archer_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = archer_captain_camp_officer }
		}

			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = archer_captain_camp_officer }

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_bloodrider_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = can_make_bloodriders } } }
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_bloodrider_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_bloodrider_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = bloodrider_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = bloodrider_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_camp_priest_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = camp_priest_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = camp_priest_camp_officer }
		}

	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_champion_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_champion_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_champion_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = champion_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = champion_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0

	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }

	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_court_artificer_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_martial_stewardship_intrigue_learning_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
		create_inspiration = { type = weapon_inspiration gold = 0 }
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_court_artificer_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = court_artificer_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = court_artificer_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes

	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }

	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
	 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_crimson_cloaks_commander_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_elephantry_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_elephantry_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_elephantry_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = elephantry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = elephantry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}

		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_first_sword_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_first_sword_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = first_sword_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = first_sword_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_foreign_emissary_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	if = {
		limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture }
		random_culture_global = {
			limit = { NOT = { this = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture } NOT = { has_cultural_pillar = language_agot_others } NOT = { has_cultural_pillar = language_agot_children } }
			save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture
		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_foreign_emissary_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_stewardship_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_foreign_emissary_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = foreign_emissary_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = foreign_emissary_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_grey_cloaks_commander_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_grey_cloaks_commander_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = grey_cloaks_commander_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = grey_cloaks_commander_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_heavy_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_heavy_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_heavy_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = heavy_cavalry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = heavy_cavalry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_high_reeve_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_high_reeve_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_learning_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_high_reeve_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = high_reeve_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = high_reeve_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_horse_archer_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_horse_archer_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_horse_archer_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = horse_archer_captain_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = horse_archer_captain_camp_officer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_household_guard_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_household_guard_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_household_guard_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = household_guard_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = household_guard_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = keeper_of_swans_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_keeper_of_the_harem_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_keeper_of_the_harem_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_intrigue_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
		if = {
			limit = { is_male = yes is_eunuch_trigger = no }
			add_trait = eunuch_1
		}
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_keeper_of_the_harem_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = keeper_of_the_harem_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = keeper_of_the_harem_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_light_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_light_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_light_cavalry_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = light_cavalry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = light_cavalry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_light_infantry_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_light_infantry_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = light_infantry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = light_infantry_captain_camp_officer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_maester_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_maester_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_learning_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne } }
			make_trait_inactive = grandmaester
		}
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_maester_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = maester_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = maester_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_master_assassin_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { has_doctrine = tenet_fp3_fedayeen NOR = { has_doctrine = tenet_pacifism has_doctrine = tenet_dharmic_pacifism } } } }
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_master_assassin_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_intrigue_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_master_assassin_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = master_assassin_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = master_assassin_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_master_of_hunt_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_learning_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_master_of_hunt_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = master_of_hunt_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = master_of_hunt_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_pike_captain_camp_officer_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = high_prowess_ignores_knight_restrictions }
				AND = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = women_can_fight_if_they_have_martial } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
			save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
		 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

		}
		else_if = {
		 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
		 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

		}
	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_pike_captain_camp_officer_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_pike_captain_camp_officer_trigger = yes }

	}

	}
}
agot_di_rusbar_spawn_royal_counter_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_royal_counter_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_stewardship_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_royal_counter_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = royal_counter_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = royal_counter_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

agot_di_rusbar_spawn_sea_watch_commander_court_position_effect = {
	save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_operator
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_target = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_employer save_scope_as = liege }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_culture }
	var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_faith }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_age value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_age }
	save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = var:agot_di_rusbar_eye_gender }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.culture = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture }
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer.faith = { save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith }
	if = {
	 limit = { scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = men_cannot_fight } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_female_only_combatant } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_culture = { has_cultural_parameter = martial_custom_male_only_combatant } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_female_if_no_roco } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 100 }

	}
	else_if = {
	 limit = { NOT = { has_dlc_feature = diverge_culture } scope:agot_di_rusbar_rule_faith = { has_doctrine_parameter = combatant_must_be_male_if_no_roco } }
	 save_scope_value_as = { name = agot_di_rusbar_female value = 0 }

	}
	create_character = {
		age = agot_di_rusbar_spawn_age
		gender_female_chance = scope:agot_di_rusbar_female
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		employer = scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer
		culture = scope:agot_di_rusbar_culture
		faith = scope:agot_di_rusbar_faith
		dynasty = none
		random_traits = no
		diplomacy = 0
		martial = 0
		stewardship = 0
		intrigue = 0
		learning = 0
		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_sea_watch_commander_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_diplomacy_martial_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_sea_watch_commander_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = sea_watch_commander_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = sea_watch_commander_court_position }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed value = yes }
		send_interface_toast = { type = event_generic_bad title = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed_title custom_tooltip = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed }
	}
}

		prowess = 0
		save_scope_as = agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier
	}
	scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = {
		save_scope_as = employee
		agot_di_rusbar_prepare_siege_engineer_court_position_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_education_martial_stewardship_learning_effect = yes
		agot_di_rusbar_court_knighthood_effect = yes
		add_trait = loyal
	}
	set_variable = { name = agot_di_rusbar_eye_last value = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier }
	remove_variable = agot_di_rusbar_eye_failed
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = { agot_di_rusbar_vacancy_siege_engineer_court_position_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier = { can_be_employed_as = siege_engineer_court_position }
		}
		scope:agot_di_rusbar_employer = {
			appoint_court_position = { recipient = scope:agot_di_rusbar_new_courtier court_position = siege_engineer_court_position }
		}
